Title: Rechenschaft unsrer Religion, Lehre und Glaubens. Von den Brüdern, die man die Huterischen nennt
Creator: Peter Riedemann (1506-1556)
Publisher: Berne, Indiana: Huterische Brüder-Gemeine (Witness Print), 1902; the publishers' note calls it an unaltered reprint ("ein unveränderter Abdruck") of the sixteenth-century printing
Publication Date: 1902
Rights: Public Domain (published 1902; public domain by date; host statement: "The Library of Congress is unaware of any copyright restrictions for this item")
Language: deu
Source: Internet Archive item rechenschaftunsr00ried, file rechenschaftunsr00ried_djvu.txt, fetched directly by the library/source-research thread (Era VII session, one-world agent for the-anabaptist-movements), 2026-10-16; host rights statement: possible-copyright-status "The Library of Congress is unaware of any copyright restrictions for this item" (contributor The Library of Congress).
Provenance note: Header prepended; the OCR text follows unchanged apart from long-s to s normalisation (0 occurrences, none present). Rights basis restated: a 1902 Indiana imprint, read off the scanned title page, is public domain by date, and the Library of Congress states it knows of no restrictions.
Content note: SECOND WITNESS for scan quality (Mark's OCR ruling, 2026-09-25): the book is set in Fraktur and the OCR is garbled throughout (sampled at five points, roughly half the word tokens are misread, e.g. "unfrer SReligion" for "unsrer Religion"); the headings and running heads are garbled too. It is vendored because it is the Hutterite Brethren's own confession of faith, the movement's fullest doctrinal statement from Moravia, and no cleaner public-domain scan was found (the microform duplicate MN5077ucmf_5 is the same OCR). Contents: a publishers' note on the Hutterite Brüder-Gemeine, then Riedemann's Vorrede and the Rechenschaft in its sections ("Von der ...") with page-numbered running heads. The English translations (London/Plough 1950; Plough 1970) are in copyright and the archive.org copies are access-restricted. Every quotation must be verified against the page images before use.
